Group Philosophy                                       集團理念

NISSIN FOODS Group is dedicated to exploring           日清食品集團致力不斷發掘及創造美味的
possibilities of food and creating food of fabulous    食物，藉此貢獻社會和地球，為大眾帶來
tastes. We contribute to society and the earth by      飲食的樂趣。
bringing people the pleasure of eating.


We call ourselves an "EARTH FOOD CREATOR" because      「EARTH FOOD CREATOR」一詞不僅意味
we are not just a food manufacturer, but also aspire   著我們是食品製造商，更包含了我們的期
to serve the world by creating food, the source of     望 ─ 透過製造被喻為生命之源的食物，服
life. "EARTH" also means land and soil, and grains,    務世界。另外，「EARTH」有土地、土壤的
which are the raw material of many foods, grow in      意思。穀物在泥土中生長，是許多食物的
soil. The mission of NISSIN FOODS Group is to create   原材料。日清食品集團的使命就是運用土
and continue to produce food with the bountiful        地賜予的資源，以創造及持續生產食物。
resources the Earth provides.

ました（年間杉の木7,400本に相当するCO2量）。                 事業運営のために、中国と香港で2022年3月1日付及
                                           び4月1日付にてそれぞれ工場出荷価格を引き上げま
第2、2020年香港製造拠点に4基の太陽光発電を設置                 した。今後は、原材料や物流に関わる高いコストを
し、2021年中国浙江省と福建省の2工場に太陽光発                  価格改定で吸収するとともに、持続的な利益成長の
電を設置しました。これにより、CO2排出量削減へ                   ためのコスト削減施策を実行していきます。
の貢献を開始しました。既存工場の節電のために、

     MANAGEMENT DISCUSSION AND ANALYSIS 管理層討論及分析

     FUTURE PROSPECTS                                                             未來展望
     Looking forward, unless and until the pandemic is contained and daily        展望未來，除非疫情得到控制且日常生活回復正
     life resumes normal, the raw material price fluctuation will continue to     常，否則原材料價格波動將繼續影響製造業務。
     impact the manufacturing businesses for the time being.


     In the PRC, the Company has taken various measures to improve                在中國，本公司已採取多項措施以提升生產效率
     production efficiency and reduce a wide range of costs in order to           及減少各類成本，以緩解價格壓力。然而，該等
     alleviate these price pressures. However, these measures are insufficient    措施不足以吸收不斷上漲的原材料成本。在這種
     to absorb the constantly rising cost of raw materials. In this challenging   具挑戰性的經濟環境下，自 2022 年 3 月 1 日起，

26   NISSIN FOODS COMPANY LIMITED   日清食品有限公司
                                                   In a small shed built in the backyard of his home, Mr. Momofuku Ando, the founder
                                                   of Nissin Foods, spent an entire year developing a ramen that can be quickly made at
                                                   home by just adding hot water. Chicken Ramen, the product of many trial and error
                                                   experiments was finally invented and launched to the market in 1958. Mr. Ando set
                                                   forth five key principles in developing Chicken Ramen, namely: 1) delicious, 2) easy
                                                   to store, 3) easy to cook, 4) reasonably priced, and 5) safe and hygienic. These very
                                                   qualities have enabled instant noodles to continue winning the hearts of people around
                                                   the world, and still hold true for Nissin Foods in developing instant noodles for today.

                                                                    在自家後院建造的小屋裡，日清食品創辦人安藤百福先生花了整整
                                                                    一年時間，研發出一種只需加入熱水就能在家快速製作的拉麵。經
                                                                    過多次反複試驗之後，雞湯拉麵最終於 1958 年發明並推出市場。
                                                                    安藤先生在開發雞湯拉麵時提出了五個大原則，即：1) 美味、2) 易
                                                                    於儲存、3) 易於烹飪、4) 價錢合理、及5) 安全衛生。正因為擁有這
                                                                    些質素，即食麵至今仍在世界各地廣受歡迎；時至今天，日清食品
                                                                    仍秉承同樣原則為大家用心製作即食麵。

Risk Management and Internal Control                                            風險管理及內部監控
The Group has formulated and adopted the risk management policy in              本集團已制定及採納風險管理政策以為識別、評
providing direction in identifying, evaluating and managing significant         估及管理主要風險提供指導。主要風險管理程序
risks. The main features of the risk management processes comprise 5            包括五個核心流程：(a) 風險確定；(b) 風險評估及
core stages: (a) risk identification, (b) risk assessment and prioritisation,   其風險優先次序；(c) 風險應對；(d) 風險監控；及
(c) risk response, (d) risk monitoring and (e) risk reporting.                  (e) 風險匯報。


The appropriate risk mitigation plans are determined based on the               本集團根據以下風險應對措施釐定適當的風險減
following risk responses:                                                       緩計劃：


–     Acceptance: Risks are considered immaterial and acceptable                －   接受：根據本集團之風險接受程度，風險被
      based on Group’s risk appetite and therefore no action is                     視為並不重大及可接受，故毋須採取任何行
      considered necessary.                                                         動。


–     Reduction: Risks cannot be considered immaterial and actions              －   降低：風險被視為不能忽視，並採取監控措
      such as controls have to be taken to reduce the impact and                    施等行動以減少影響及風險承受程度至可接
      vulnerability to an acceptable level.                                         受水平。


–     Sharing: Risks cannot be considered immaterial and the Group              －   分擔：風險被視為不能忽視，本集團本身無
      itself cannot effectively reduce the risks to an acceptably low               法自行將風險有效降至可接受的水平，故須
      level, therefore a portion of the risks has to be transferred to or           透過保險及外判等方式將部分風險轉移至或
      shared with other parties by insurance, outsourcing, etc.                     與其他各方共同分擔。


–     Avoidance: Risks are so significant that there is no measure              －   防範：風險被視為相當重大，本集團沒有足
      both internally and externally to reduce the risk to an acceptable            夠及有效的內部監管系統及外界措施可將風
      level, or involve unreasonably high cost to reduce the risk to                險降至可接受水平或需不合理及高昂之成本
      an acceptable level. Therefore, activities giving rise to the risks           將風險降至可接受水平。因此，須避開產生
      should be avoided.                                                            有關風險之活動。


The Group has adopted the COSO ERM Framework, the “Top-Down”                    本集團已採納 COSO 的風險管理框架（
                                                                                                   「由上而下」
approach, in establishing its risk management framework, which                  方式）由董事會、審核委員會及風險管理專責小組
consists of the Board, the Audit Committee, the Risk Management                 及各有關部門組成的風險管理及內部監控架構。
Taskforce and various departments within the Group.


The Board acknowledges that it is responsible for the risk management           董事會深明其負責風險管理及內部監控系統，並
and internal control systems and reviewing their effectiveness. The             檢討其成效。董事會釐定達致本集團業務目標所

effectiveness of risk management and internal control systems.

     (i)    Relationship with Customers                                            (i)    與客戶之關係
            The Group places a strong emphasis on establishing and                        本集團極其重視與客戶建立及維持良好而穩
            maintaining solid and stable business relationships with                      定的業務關係，致力提供安全、美味、獨特

            differentiated and trendy products. It also stays connected with              市場推廣資訊及優惠以及社交網絡等渠道，
            customers through channels like the Company’s homepage,                       與客戶保持聯繫從而緊貼消費者不斷轉變之
            promotions, marketing materials and incentives, and social media              喜好。
            to keep abreast of the changing consumer preferences.


     (ii)   Relationship with Distributors                                         (ii)   與分銷商之關係
            The Group has developed a strong distribution network which                   本集團已發展強大分銷網絡，並藉此保持市
            in turn enables it to maintain a leading position in the market.              場領先地位。為了與其分銷商維持長期而穩
            In order to maintain a long term and stable business relationship             定的業務關係，本集團與其主要分銷商訂立
            with its distributors, the Group enters into distribution agreements          分銷協議，據此，分銷商不時向本集團發出
            with its major distributors under which the distributors place                一般載有將予採購產品種類、價格及數量的
            purchase order from time to time that typically set out the types,            採購訂單。
            prices and quantities of products to be purchased.


     (iii) Relationship with Suppliers                                             (iii) 與供應商之關係
            The Group procures a majority of raw materials from independent               本集團向國內及海外的獨立第三方供應商採
            third-party domestic and overseas suppliers. It has different                 購大部分原材料。為免過度倚賴單一供應
            suppliers for each type of raw materials for both Hong Kong and               商，本集團在香港及中國的營運就每種原材
            the PRC operation to avoid overreliance on any single supplier.               料聘有不同供應商。


            Meanwhile, the Group procures a portion of raw materials, such as             與此同時，本集團向日清日本採購湯底、調
            a soup base, seasoning and condiments from Nissin Japan with                  味料及佐料等部分原材料，並相信日清日本
            the belief that it has better quality control and some of the raw             集團具有較好的質量控制及部分原材料是獨
            materials are unique.                                                         一無二的。


            The Group also carefully selects its suppliers by evaluating                  在審慎甄選供應商時，本集團會評估彼等之
            their overall track record, financial strengths, reliability,                 整體往績記錄、財務實力、可靠性、競爭力、
            competitiveness, stability of supply, quality control measures,               供貨穩定性、質量控制措施、定價及物流安

OPINION                                                                    意見
We have audited the consolidated financial statements of Nissin Foods      本核數師（以下簡稱「我們」）已審計列載於第83至
Company Limited (the “Company”) and its subsidiaries (collectively         223頁的日清食品有限公司（

                                                                                   ）的綜合財務報表，此綜合財務
comprise the consolidated statement of financial position as at 31         報表包括於2021年12月31日的綜合財務狀況表與
December 2021, and the consolidated statement of profit or loss and        截至該日止年度的綜合損益及其他全面收入表、
other comprehensive income, consolidated statement of changes in           綜合權益變動表和綜合現金流量表，以及綜合財

ended, and notes to the consolidated financial statements, including a

In our opinion, the consolidated financial statements give a true and      我們認為，該等綜合財務報表已根據香港會計
fair view of the consolidated financial position of the Group as at 31     師公會（「香港會計師公會」）頒佈的香港財務報告

with Hong Kong Financial Reporting Standards (“HKFRSs”) issued by          及截至該日止年度的綜合財務表現及綜合現金流
the Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public Accountants (“HKICPA”)         量，並已遵照香港公司條例妥為擬備。

BASIS FOR OPINION                                                          意見的基礎
We conducted our audit in accordance with Hong Kong Standards on           我們已根據香港會計師公會頒佈的香港審計準則
Auditing (“HKSAs”) issued by the HKICPA. Our responsibilities under       （「香港審計準則」）進行審計。我們在該等準則下
those standards are further described in the Auditor’s Responsibilities    承擔的責任已在本報告「核數師就審計綜合財務報
for the Audit of the Consolidated Financial Statements section of our      表承擔的責任」部分中作進一步闡述。根據香港會
report. We are independent of the Group in accordance with the             計師公會頒佈的專業會計師道德守則（「守則」），
HKICPA’s Code of Ethics for Professional Accountants (the “Code”),         我們獨立於         貴集團，並已履行守則中的其他專
and we have fulfilled our other ethical responsibilities in accordance     業道德責任。我們相信，我們所獲得的審計憑證
with the Code. We believe that the audit evidence we have obtained is      能充足及適當地為我們的審計意見提供基礎。
sufficient and appropriate to provide a basis for our opinion.

     1.   GENERAL INFORMATION                                                   1.   一般資料
          Nissin Foods Company Limited (the “Company”) is a public                   日清食品有限公司（
                                                                                             「本公司」
                                                                                                 ）為一間於香港
          limited company incorporated in Hong Kong and its shares                   註冊成立之公眾有限公司，其股份於香港聯
          are listed on The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited. Its                 合交易所有限公司上市。其直接及最終控股
          immediate and ultimate holding company is Nissin Foods                     公司為Nissin Foods Holdings Co., Ltd.（一間
          Holdings Co., Ltd., a company incorporated in Japan with its               於日本註冊成立且其股份於東京證券交易所
          shares listed on the Tokyo Stock Exchange.                                 上市之公司）。


          The addresses of the registered office and principal place of              本公司之註冊辦事處地址位於香港新界大埔
          business of the Company are 21-23 Dai Shing Street, Tai Po                 大埔工業邨大盛街21–23號，主要營業地點
          Industrial Estate, Tai Po, New Territories, Hong Kong, and 11-13           位於香港新界大埔大埔工業邨大順街11–13
          Dai Shun Street, Tai Po Industrial Estate, Tai Po, New Territories,        號。
          Hong Kong, respectively.


          The Company and its subsidiaries (collectively referred to as the          本公司及其附屬公司（統稱「本集團」
                                                                                                     ）主要從
          “Group”) is principally engaged in the manufacturing and sales             事生產及銷售麵條、蒸煮食品、冷凍食品、
          of noodles, retort foods, frozen foods, beverage products, snacks          飲料產品、零食及蔬菜產品以及提供研究及
          and vegetable products, and provision of research and publicity            宣傳服務。
          services.


          The consolidated financial statements are presented in Hong                綜合財務報表以本公司功能貨幣港元（「港
          Kong dollars (“HK$”), which is also the functional currency of the         元」
                                                                                      ）呈列。
          Company.


     2.   APPLICATION OF AMENDMENTS TO HONG KONG FINANCIAL                      2.   應用香港財務報告準則（「香
          REPORTING STANDARDS (“HKFRSs”)                                             港財務報告準則」）的修訂
          Amendments to HKFRSs that are mandatorily                                  於本年度強制生效之經修訂香港財務報
          effective for the current year                                             告準則

     In addition, the Group applied the agenda decision of the IFRS              此外，本集團已應用國際財務報告準則詮釋
     Interpretations Committee (the “Committee”) of the International            委員會（
                                                                                    「委員會」
                                                                                        ）於2021年6月頒布的議程
     Accounting Standards Board issued in June 2021 which clarified              決定，澄清實體於釐定存貨的可變現淨值時
     the costs an entity should include as “estimated costs necessary            應將成本計入「銷售必要的估計成本」。
     to make the sale” when determining the net realisable value of
     inventories.


     Except as described below, the application of the amendments                除下文所述者外，於本年度應用香港財務報
     to HKFRSs in the current year has had no material impact on the             告準則之修訂對本集團本年度及過往年度的
     Group’s financial positions and performance for the current and             財務狀況及表現及╱或該等綜合財務報表所
     prior years and/or on the disclosures set out in these consolidated         載之披露並無重大影響。
     financial statements.


     Impacts on application of the agenda decision of the                        委員會議程決定 — 出售存貨的必要成
     Committee – Cost necessary to sell inventories (HKAS                        本的影響（香港會計準則第2號存貨）
     2 Inventories)
     In June 2021, the Committee, through its agenda decision,                   於2021年6月，委員會通過其議程決定澄清
     clarified the costs an entity should include as “estimated costs            實體於釐定存貨的可變現淨值時應將成本計
     necessary to make the sale” when determining the net realisable             入「銷售必要的估計成本」。特別是，此類成
     value of inventories. In particular, whether such costs should be           本是否應僅限於銷售增量的成本。委員會認
     limited to those that are incremental to the sale. The Committee            為，銷售必要的估計成本不應限於增量成
     concluded that the estimated costs necessary to make the sale               本，還應包括實體出售其存貨必須承擔的成
     should not be limited to those that are incremental but should              本，包括非特定銷售增量的成本。
     also include costs that an entity must incur to sell its inventories
     including those that are not incremental to a particular sale.


     The Group’s accounting policy prior to the Committee’s agenda               於委員會作出議程決定前，本集團的會計政
     decision was to determine the net realisable value of inventories           策為於釐定存貨的可變現淨值時僅考慮增量
     taking into consideration incremental costs only. Upon application          成本。於應用委員會的議程決定後，本集團
     of the Committee’s agenda decision, the Group changed its                   對其會計政策作出更改，於釐定存貨的可變
     accounting policy to determine the net realisable value of                  現淨值時同時考慮增量成本及促銷開支。新
     inventories taking into consideration both incremental costs and            會計政策已追溯應用。
     promotion expenses. The new accounting policy has been applied
     retrospectively.


     The application of the Committee’s agenda decision has had                  應用委員會的議程決定對本集團的財務狀況
     no material impact on the Group’s financial positions and                   及表現並無重大影響。
     performance.

     HKAS 1 is amended to replace all instances of the term                       香港會計準則第1號修訂以「重大會計政策
     “significant accounting policies” with “material accounting policy           資料」取代「主要會計政策」一詞的所有情
     information”. Accounting policy information is material if, when             況。倘連同實體財務報表內其他資料一併考
     considered together with other information included in an entity’s           慮，會計政策資料可以合理預期會影響通用
     financial statements, it can reasonably be expected to influence             財務報表的主要使用者根據該等財務報表所
     decisions that are primary users of general purpose financial                作出的決定，則該會計政策資料屬重大。
     statements make on the basis of those financial statements.


     The amendments also clarify that accounting policy information               該等修訂亦澄清，即使涉及款項並不重大，
     may be material because of the nature of the related transactions,           但基於相關交易性質、其他事項或情況，會
     other events or conditions, even if the amounts are immaterial.              計政策資料仍可屬重大。然而，並非所有與
     However, not all accounting policy information relating to                   重大交易、其他事項或情況有關的會計政策
     material transactions, other events or conditions is itself material.        資料本身即屬重大。倘一間實體選擇披露非
     If an entity chooses to disclose immaterial accounting policy                重大會計政策資料，有關資料不得掩蓋重大
     information, such information must not obscure material                      會計政策資料。
     accounting policy information.

          Amendments to HKAS 8 Definition of Accounting                                香港會計準則第8號（修訂本）會計估計
          Estimates                                                                    的定義
          The amendments define accounting estimates as “monetary                      該修訂定義會計估計為「存在計量不明朗因
          amounts in financial statements that are subject to measurement              素的財務報表之貨幣金額」。會計政策可能
          uncertainty”. An accounting policy may require items in financial            規定對計量不明朗因素的財務報表的項目進
          statements to be measured in a way that involves measurement                 行計量－即會計政策可能規定將按貨幣金額
          uncertainty-that is, the accounting policy may require such items            計量的有關項目不可直接觀察而須予以估
          to be measured at monetary amounts that cannot be observed                   計。於此情況下，一間實體應編製會計估
          directly and must instead be estimated. In such a case, an entity            計，旨在達到會計政策載列的目標。編製會
          develops an accounting estimate to achieve the objective set                 計估計包括根據最新可得可靠的資料作出的
          out by the accounting policy. Developing accounting estimates                判斷或假設用途。
          involves the use of judgements or assumptions based on the
          latest available, reliable information.


          In addition, the concept of changes in accounting estimates in               此外，香港會計準則第8號的會計估計變更
          HKAS 8 is retained with additional clarifications.                           的概念予以保留，惟有進一步澄清。

                 The consolidated financial statements have been prepared                 除下文會計政策所詳述若干於各報告
                 on the historical cost basis except for certain financial                期末按公平值計量之金融工具外，綜

                 of each reporting period, as explained in the accounting
                 policies set out below.


                 Historical cost is generally based on the fair value of the              歷史成本通常基於交換貨品及服務時
                 consideration given in exchange for goods and services.                  所提供代價之公平值。

                 asset or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction          秩序交易中出售資產可收取或轉讓負
                 between market participants at the measurement date,                     債須支付之價格，而不論該價格是否
                 regardless of whether that price is directly observable or               直接可觀察或可使用其他估值技術估
                 estimated using another valuation technique. In estimating               計。倘市場參與者於計量日期對資產
                 the fair value of an asset or a liability, the Group takes into          或負債定價時會考慮資產或負債之特
                 account the characteristics of the asset or liability if market          點，則本集團於估計資產或負債之公
                 participants would take those characteristics into account               平值時會考慮該等特點。該等綜合財
                 when pricing the asset or liability at the measurement date.             務報表中作計量及╱或披露用途之公
                 Fair value for measurement and/or disclosure purposes in                 平值乃按此基準釐定，惟屬於香港財
                 these consolidated financial statements is determined on                 務報告準則第2號以股份為基礎付款
                 such a basis, except for share-based payment transactions                範圍內之以股份為基礎付款交易、根
                 that are within the scope of HKFRS 2 Share-based                         據香港財務報告準則第16號入賬的租
                 Payment , leasing transactions that are accounted for in                 賃交易，以及與公平值有部分相若地
                 accordance with HKFRS 16, and measurements that have                     方但並非公平值之計量，譬如香港會
                 some similarities to fair value but are not fair value, such as          計準則第2號存貨 內之可變現淨值或
                 net realisable value in HKAS 2 Inventories or value in use in            香港會計準則第36號資產減值 之使用
                 HKAS 36 Impairment of Assets .                                           價值除外。


                 For financial instruments which are transacted at fair value             就按公平值交易的金融工具及於其後
                 and a valuation technique that unobservable inputs is                    期間使用不可觀察輸入數據計量公平

                 date when the Company ceases to control the subsidiary.

                 are attributed to the owners of the Company and to the                 於本公司擁有人及非控制性權益。附
                 non-controlling interests. Total comprehensive income of               屬公司之全面收入額總歸屬於本公司
                 subsidiaries is attributed to the owners of the Company                擁有人及非控制性權益，即使此舉會
                 and to the non-controlling interests even if this results in           導致非控制性權益產生虧絀結餘。
                 the non-controlling interests having a deficit balance.

                 in line with the Group’s accounting policies.                          之會計政策一致。

          transaction basis, that permits a simplified assessment of                   性測試，其允許對所收購的一組活動
          whether an acquired set of activities and assets is not a                    及資產是否構成一項業務進行簡化評
          business. The concentration test is met if substantially all of              估。倘所收購的總資產的絕大部分公
          the fair value of the gross assets acquired is concentrated                  平值均集中於單一可識別資產或一組
          in a single identifiable asset or group of similar identifiable              類似可識別資產中，則符合集中性測
          assets. The gross assets under assessment exclude cash                       試。接受評估的總資產不包括現金及
          and cash equivalents, deferred tax assets, and goodwill                      現金等價物、遞延稅項資產及遞延稅
          resulting from the effects of deferred tax liabilities. If the               項負債的影響所產生的商譽。倘符合
          concentration test is met, the set of activities and assets is               集中性測試，該組活動及資產被釐定
          determined not to be a business and no further assessment                    為並非一項業務及毋須作進一步評
          is needed.                                                                   估。


          Asset acquisitions                                                           資產收購
          When the Group acquires a group of assets and liabilities                    當本集團收購一組資產及負債不構成
          that do not constitute a business, the Group identifies and                  一項業務時，本集團首先透過按金
          recognises the individual identifiable assets acquired and                   融資產╱金融負債各自的公平值分配
          liabilities assumed by allocating the purchase price first                   購買價，以識別及確認取得的個別可
          to financial assets/financial liabilities at the respective fair             識別資產及承擔的負債，餘下購買價
          values, the remaining balance of the purchase price is then                  結餘其後按各自於購買日期的相對公
          allocated to the other identifiable assets and liabilities on                平值分配至其他可識別資產及負債。
          the basis of their relative fair values at the date of purchase.             該等交易不會產生商譽或議價購買收
          Such a transaction does not give rise to goodwill or bargain                 益。
          purchase gain.


          Business combinations                                                        業務合併
          Acquisitions of businesses are accounted for using the                       收購業務採用收購法入賬。業務合併
          acquisition method. The consideration transferred in a                       所轉讓代價按公平值計量，而計算方
          business combination is measured at fair value, which is                     法為本集團所轉讓資產、本集團向收
          calculated as the sum of the acquisition-date fair values of                 購對象原擁有人產生的負債及本集團
          the assets transferred by the Group, liabilities incurred by                 為交換收購對象控制權發行的股權於
          the Group to the former owners of the acquiree and the                       收購日的公平值總額。有關收購的成
          equity interests issued by the Group in exchange for control                 本一般於產生時在損益確認。
          of the acquiree. Acquisition-related costs are generally
          recognised in profit or loss as incurred.

                 segment.

                 group of cash-generating units).

          Investment in an associate                                                 於聯營公司的投資
          An associate is an entity over which the Group has                         聯營公司為本集團對其擁有重大影響

          policies.

          incorporated in these consolidated financial statements                    益會計法綜合計入該等綜合財務報

          purposes are prepared using uniform accounting policies                    下就類似交易及事件採用之會計政策
          as those of the Group for like transactions and events                     一致者編製。根據權益法，於聯營公
          in similar circumstances. Under the equity method, an                      司之投資初步按成本於綜合財務狀況

          adjusted thereafter to recognise the Group’s share of the                  入。聯營公司資產淨值之變動（損益
          profit or loss and other comprehensive income of the                       及其他全面收入除外）不予入賬，除
          associate. Changes in net assets of the associate other than               非該等變動導致本集團於聯營公司所
          profit or loss and other comprehensive income are not                      持擁有權權益出現變動。當本集團分
          accounted for unless such changes resulted in changes in                   佔聯營公司之虧損超出其於該聯營公
          ownership interest held by the Group. When the Group’s                     司之權益，則本集團不再繼續確認其

          in that associate, the Group discontinues recognising its                  僅以本集團已產生法定或推定責任或

          only to the extent that the Group has incurred legal or

                 Output method                                                          輸出法
                 The progress towards complete satisfaction of a                        完全達成履約責任的進度按輸出法計
                 performance obligation is measured based on output                     量，該輸出法根據直接計量至今已轉
                 method, which is to recognise revenue on the basis of                  移予客戶的貨品或服務價值與該合約
                 direct measurements of the value of the goods or services              項下承諾的餘下貨品或服務相比較確
                 transferred to customers to date relative to the remaining             認收益，可以最佳方式描述本集團轉
                 goods or services promised under the contract, that best               移貨品或服務控制權的履約情況。
                 depict the Group’s performance in transferring control of
                 goods or services.


                 As a practical expedient, if the Group has a right to                  作為一項可行權宜方法，倘本集團有
                 consideration in an amount that corresponds directly                   權收取與本集團至今已完成履約價值
                 with the value of the Group’s performance completed to                 （即本集團就所提供服務每月固定金
                 date (i.e. service contracts in which the Group bills a fixed          額的服務合約）直接對應的金額，則
                 amount for each month of service provided), the Group                  本集團有權按本集團有權發票的金額
                 recognises revenue in an amount to which the Group has                 確認收益。
                 the right to invoice.


                 Variable consideration                                                 可變代價
                 For contracts that contain variable consideration, such                就訂有可變代價（如銷售回贈）之合約
                 as sales rebate, the Group estimates the amount of                     而言，本集團以(a)預期價值法；或(b)
                 consideration to which it will be entitled using either (a)            最有可能獲得之金額估計其可獲得之
                 the expected value method or (b) the most likely amount,               代價金額，取於那方法較佳預測本集
                 depending on which method better predicts the amount of                團可獲得之代價金額。
                 consideration to which the Group will be entitled.


                 The estimated amount of variable consideration is                      可變代價之估計金額乃包含於交易價
                 included in the transaction price only to the extent that              格內，惟僅當可變代價之相關不確
                 it is highly probable that such an inclusion will not result           定性在其後變得確定，致使有關估計
                 in a significant revenue reversal in the future when the               金額很大可能不會導致於將來出現重
                 uncertainty associated with the variable consideration is              大收益撥回，其方可包含於交易價格
                 subsequently resolved.                                                 內。

                and are presented as right to refunded goods assets.                         整）。

      3.   BASIS OF PREPARATION OF CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL                        3.   綜合財務報表編製基準及主
           STATEMENTS AND SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES (continued)                 要會計政策（續）
           3.2 Significant accounting policies (continued)                                     （續）
                                                                                      3.2 主要會計政策
                 Leases                                                                 租賃
                 Definition of a lease                                                  租賃的定義
                 A contract is, or contains, a lease if the contract conveys            倘合約為換取代價而給予在一段時間
                 the right to control the use of an identified asset for a              內控制可識別資產使用的權利，則該
                 period of time in exchange for consideration.                          合約是租賃或包含租賃。


                 For contracts entered into or modified on or after the date            就於首次應用日期或之後或因業務合
                 of initial application or arising from business combinations,          併而訂立或修改的合約而言，本集團
                 the Group assesses whether a contract is or contains a lease           根據香港財務報告準則第16號的定義
                 based on the definition under HKFRS 16 at inception or                 於初始或修改日期（如適用）評估該合
                 modification date, as appropriate. Such contract will not be           約是否為租賃或包含租賃。除非合約
                 reassessed unless the terms and conditions of the contract             的條款及條件其後出現變動，否則有
                 are subsequently changed. As a practical expedient, leases             關合約將不予重新評估。作為簡易實
                 with similar characteristics are accounted on a portfolio              務處理方法，當本集團合理預期對綜
                 basis when the Group reasonably expects that the effects               合財務報表的影響將不會與組合內的
                 on the consolidated financial statements would not differ              個別租賃顯著不同時，具有類似特徵
                 materially from individual leases within the portfolio.                的租賃按組合基準入賬。


                 The Group as a lessee                                                  本集團作為承租人
                 Allocation of consideration to components of a contract                將代價分配至合約的組成部分
                 For a contract that contains a lease component and one or              就包含租賃組成部分以及一項或多項
                 more additional lease or non-lease components, the Group               額外租賃或非租賃組成部分的合約而
                 allocates the consideration in the contract to each lease              言，本集團依照租賃組成部分的相對
                 component on the basis of the relative stand-alone price of            獨立價格及非租賃組成部分的總獨立
                 the lease component and the aggregate stand-alone price                價格，將合約中的代價分配至各組賃
                 of the non-lease components.                                           組成部分。


                 Non-lease components are separated from lease                          非租賃部份獨立於租賃部份，並應用
                 component and are accounted for by applying other                      其他適用準則處理。
                 applicable standards.


                 Short-term leases                                                      短期租賃
                 The Group applies the short-term lease recognition                     本集團對自開始日期起計租期為12個
                 exemption to leases of staff quarters that have a lease term           月或以下且不包含購買選擇權的員工
                 of 12 months or less from the commencement date and do                 宿舍租賃應用短期租賃的確認豁免。
                 not contain a purchase option. Lease payments on short-                短期租賃的租賃付款在租賃期內採用
                 term leases are recognised as expense on a straight-line               直線基準或其他系統性基準確認為支
                 basis or another systematic basis over the lease term.                 出。

          Right-of-use assets                                                      使用權資產
          The cost of right-of-use asset includes:                                 使用權資產的成本包括：


          •     the amount of the initial measurement of the lease                 •        租賃負債的初步計量金額；
                liability;


          •     any lease payments made at or before the                           •        於開始日期或之前作出的任何
                commencement date, less any lease incentives                                租賃付款，減任何已收租賃優
                received;                                                                   惠；


          •     any indirect costs incurred by the Group; and                      •        本集團產生的任何直接成本；
                                                                                            及


          •     an estimated costs to be incurred by the Group in                  •        本集團於拆解及搬遷相關資
                dismantling and removing the underlying assets,                             產、復原相關資產所在場地或
                restoring the site on which it is located or restoring                      復原相關資產至租賃的條款及
                the underlying asset to the condition required by the                       條件所規定的狀況時產生的成
                terms and conditions of the lease.                                          本估計。

          accumulated depreciation and impairment losses, and                      及減值虧損計量，並就租賃負債的任
          adjusted for any remeasurement of lease liabilities.                     何重新計量作出調整。


          Right-of-use assets in which Group is reasonably certain to              本集團於租期屆滿時合理確定獲得相
          obtain ownership of the underlying leased assets at the end              關租賃資產的所有權的使用權資產
          of the lease term are depreciated from commencement                      於開始日期至使用期結束內折舊。否
          date to the end of the useful life. Otherwise, right-of-use              則，使用權資產於其估計使用期及租
          assets are depreciated on a straight-line basis over the                 期（以較短者為準）內按直線法基準折
          shorter of its estimated useful life and the lease term.                 舊。


          The Group presents right-of-use assets as a separate line                本集團於綜合財務狀況表內將使用權
          item on the consolidated statement of financial position.                資產呈列為個別項目。

          transferred to profit or loss on a systematic and rational
          basis over the useful lives of the related assets.


          Government grants related to income that are receivable as                作為已產生開支或虧損之補償或用作
          compensation for expenses or losses already incurred or for               為本集團提供即時財務支持而無未來

          Group with no future related costs are recognised in profit               於應收期間在損益內確認。該等補貼

                 Income tax expense represents the sum of the tax currently             所得稅開支指即期應付稅項及遞延稅
                 payable and deferred tax.                                              項之總和。


                 The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit                   即期應付稅項按年內應課稅溢利計
                 for the year. Taxable profit differs from profit before                算。應課稅溢利與「除稅前溢利」不
                 taxation because of income or expense that are taxable                 同，乃由於其他年度之應課稅或可扣
                 or deductible in other years and items that are never                  稅收入或開支以及從毋須課稅或不能
                 taxable or deductible. The Group’s liability for current tax           扣稅之項目。本集團之即期稅項負債
                 is calculated using tax rates that have been enacted or                乃根據於報告期末已頒佈或實質上已
                 substantively enacted by the end of the reporting period.              頒佈之稅率計算。

                 between the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities in the          負債賬面值與計算應課稅溢利所用
                 consolidated financial statements and the corresponding                相應稅基之暫時性差額確認。遞延稅
                 tax bases used in the computation of taxable profit.                   項負債一般就所有應課稅暫時性差額
                 Deferred tax liabilities are generally recognised for                  確認。遞延稅項資產一般會於可能出
                 all taxable temporary differences. Deferred tax assets                 現應課稅溢利以抵銷此等可扣稅之暫
                 are generally recognised for all deductible temporary                  時性差額時就所有可扣稅之暫時性差
                 differences to the extent that it is probable that taxable             額確認。倘暫時性差額源自初步確認
                 profits will be available against which those deductible               （除業務合併外）不影響應課稅溢利或
                 temporary differences can be utilised. Such deferred tax               會計溢利之交易之資產及負債，則有
                 assets and liabilities are not recognised if the temporary             關遞延稅項資產及負債不會予以確
                 difference arises from the initial recognition (other than             認。此外，倘於初步確認商譽後產生
                 in a business combination) of assets and liabilities in a              暫時性差額，遞延稅項負債將不會予
                 transaction that affects neither the taxable profit nor the            以遞延。
                 accounting profit. In addition, deferred tax liabilities are
                 not recognised if the temporary difference arises from the
                 initial recognition of goodwill.

                 differences associated with investments in subsidiaries and            公司投資相關之應課稅暫時性差額
                 an associate, except where the Group is able to control the            確認，惟本集團可控制有關暫時性差
                 reversal of the temporary difference and it is probable that           額撥回且於可見將來可能不會撥回除

                 future. Deferred tax assets arising from deductible                    銷暫時性差額之抵免且預期暫時性差

                 the benefits of the temporary differences and they are

          Inventories                                                                 存貨
          Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net                         存貨按成本與可變現淨值兩者之較低
          realisable value. Costs of inventories are determined on a                  者列賬。存貨成本按「先入先出」方法
          first-in, first-out method. Net realisable value represents                 釐定。可變現淨值指存貨之估計售價
          the estimated selling price for inventories less all estimated              減全部估計完成成本及進行出售之必
          costs of completion and costs necessary to make the sale.                   要成本。銷售所需的成本包括直接歸
          Costs necessary to make the sale include incremental costs                  屬於銷售的增量成本和公司進行銷售
          directly attributable to the sale and non-incremental costs                 所必須產生的非增量成本。
          which the Group must incur to make the sale.


          Provisions                                                                  撥備
          Provisions are recognised when the Group has a present                      撥備於本集團由於過往事件而產生現
          obligation (legal or constructive) as a result of a past event,             有的法定或推定責任，本集團可能須
          it is probable that the Group will be required to settle that               解決有關責任，以及能夠可靠地估計
          obligation, and a reliable estimate can be made of the                      解決有關責任的金額時確認。
          amount of the obligation.


          The amount recognised as a provision is the best estimate                   確認為撥備之金額乃於報告期末對解
          of the consideration required to settle the present                         決當前責任所需代價的最佳估計，並
          obligation at the end of the reporting period, taking                       計及有關責任所涉及的風險及不確定
          into account the risks and uncertainties surrounding the                    因素。倘撥備乃按估計用以償付當前
          obligation. When a provision is measured using the cash                     責任所需現金流量計算，其賬面值為
          flows estimated to settle the present obligation, its carrying              該等現金流量之現值（倘金錢時間價
          amount is the present value of those cash flows (where the                  值的影響為重大）。
          effect of the time value of money is material).


          Contingent liabilities                                                      或然負債
          A contingent liability is a present obligation arising from                 或然負債指因過往事件引致之現時責
          past events but is not recognised because it is not probable                任（但其因需要流出具有經濟利益之
          that an outflow of resources embodying economic benefits                    資源以履行責任之機會不大或責任
          will be required to settle the obligation or the amount                     金額不能足夠可靠地計量而不予確
          of the obligation cannot be measured with sufficient                        認）。
          reliability.


          Where the Group is jointly and severally liable for an                      如本集團對須共同及各別承擔某項責
          obligation, the part of the obligation that is expected to be               任，該項責任中預期由另一方承擔的
          met by other parties is treated as a contingent liability and               部分則以或然負債處理，並不須綜合
          it is not recognised in the consolidated financial statements.              財務報表中予以確認。

          Financial instruments (continued)                                            金融工具（續）
          The effective interest method is a method of calculating                     實際利息法為計算金融資產或金融負
          the amortised cost of a financial asset or financial liability               債攤銷成本及於有關期間內攤分利
          and of allocating interest income and interest expense over                  息收入及利息開支的方法。實際利率
          the relevant period. The effective interest rate is the rate                 是按金融資產或金融負債預計年期或
          that exactly discounts estimated future cash receipts and                   （倘適用）較短期間將估計未來現金收
          payments (including all fees and points paid or received                     入及付款（包括所支付或收取構成實
          that form an integral part of the effective interest rate,                   際利率組成部分的所有費用及點子、
          transaction costs and other premiums or discounts) through                   交易成本及其他溢價或折讓）準確貼
          the expected life of the financial asset or financial liability,             現至初步確認時的賬面淨額的比率。
          or, where appropriate, a shorter period, to the net carrying
          amount on initial recognition.


          Financial assets                                                             金融資產
          Classification and subsequent measurement of financial                       金融資產分類及後續計量
          assets

          subsequently measured at amortised cost:                                     成本計量：


          •        the financial asset is held within a business model                 •        以收取合約現金流量為目的之
                   whose objective is to collect contractual cash flows;                        業務模式下持有的金融資產；
                   and                                                                          及


          •        the contractual terms give rise on specified dates to               •        合約條款引致於指定日期的現
                   cash flows that are solely payments of principal and                         金流量僅為支付本金及未償還
                   interest on the principal amount outstanding.                                的本金利息。


          Financial assets that meet the following conditions are                      符合下列條件的金融資產其後按公平
          subsequently measured at fair value through other                            值計入其他全面收入（
                                                                                                「按公平值計入
          comprehensive income (“FVTOCI”):                                             其他全面收入」
                                                                                             ）計量：


          •        the financial asset is held within a business model                 •        以收取合約現金流量及出售達
                   whose objective is achieved by both selling and                              致目標的業務模式下持有的金
                   collecting contractual cash flows; and                                       融資產；及


          •        the contractual terms give rise on specified dates to               •        合約條款引致於指定日期的現
                   cash flows that are solely payments of principal and                         金流量僅為支付本金及未償還
                   interest on the principal amount outstanding.                                的本金利息。

                 The Group performs impairment assessment under                            本集團根據香港財務報告準則第9號
                 expected credit loss (“ECL”) model on financial assets                    須予減值的金融資產，按預期信貸
                 (including trade receivables, other receivables, loan                     虧損（「預期信貸虧損」）模式進行減
                 receivable, amounts due from ultimate holding company                     值（包括貿易應收賬款、其他應收款

                 with maturity over three months, time deposits with                       同系附屬公司款項、應收債券、三個
                 maturity over one year and bank balances) which are                       月以上定期存款、一年以上定期存款
                 subject to impairment assessment under HKFRS 9. The                       及銀行結餘）的預期信貸虧損確認虧
                 amount of ECL is updated at each reporting date to reflect                損撥備。預期信貸虧損金額於各報告
                 changes in credit risk since initial recognition.                         日期更新，以反映自初始確認以來的
                                                                                           信貸風險變動。

                 Lifetime ECL represents the ECL that will result from                     全期預期信貸虧損指相關工具於預計
                 all possible default events over the expected life of the                 全期內所有可能違約事件所產生的預
                 relevant instrument. In contrast, 12-month ECL (“12m                      期信貸虧損。相反，12個月預期信貸
                 ECL”) represents the portion of lifetime ECL that is                      虧損（
                                                                                             「12個月預期信貸虧損」
                                                                                                        ）指預計
                 expected to result from default events that are possible                  於報告日期後12個月內可能發生的
                 within 12 months after the reporting date. Assessments are                違約事件產生的部分全期預期信貸虧
                 done based on the Group’s historical credit loss experience,              損。評估乃根據本集團過往信貸虧損
                 adjusted for factors that are specific to the debtors, general            經驗，並就債務人特定因素、一般經
                 economic conditions and an assessment of both the current                 濟狀況以及對於報告日期之當時狀況
                 conditions at the reporting date as well as the forecast of               及未來狀況預測的評估作調整。
                 future conditions.

                 The Group always recognises lifetime ECL for trade                        本集團一直確認貿易應收賬款及應收
                 receivables and amounts due from fellow subsidiaries                      同系附屬公司款項（屬貿易性質）之全
                 which are in trade nature. The ECL on these assets are                    期預期信貸虧損。該等資產的預期信
                 assessed individually for debtors with significant balances               貸虧損乃就具有重大結餘的債務人進
                 and collectively using a provision matrix with appropriate                行個別評估及使用合適分組的撥備矩
                 groupings. The amounts due from subsidiaries which are in                 陣進行整體評估。應收附屬公司款項
                 trade nature are assessed individually.                                   （屬貿易性質）乃個別評估。

                 For all other instruments, the Group measures the loss                    就所有其他工具而言，本集團計量虧
                 allowance equal to 12m ECL, unless when there has been                    損撥備等於12個月預期信貸虧損，
                 a significant increase in credit risk since initial recognition,          除非當信貸風險自初始確認以來顯著
                 in which case the Group recognises lifetime ECL. The                      上升，則本集團確認全期預期信貸虧
                 assessment of whether lifetime ECL should be recognised is                損。評估是否應確認全期預期信貸虧
                 based on significant increases in the likelihood or risk of a             損乃根據自初始確認以來發生違約的
                 default occurring since initial recognition.                              可能性或風險顯著上升。

                         is information indicating that the counterparty is                        財政困難，且並無實際收回款
                         in severe financial difficulty and there is no realistic                  項的可能時（例如對方被清盤或
                         prospect of recovery, for example, when the                               進入破產程序或（倘為貿易應收
                         counterparty has been placed under liquidation or                         賬款）款項逾期超過一年（以較
                         has entered into bankruptcy proceedings, or in the                        早發生者為準）)，本集團會撇
                         case of trade receivables, when the amounts are                           銷金融資產。經計及在適當情
                         over one year past due, whichever occurs sooner.                          況下的法律意見後，已撇銷的
                         Financial assets written off may still be subject to                      金融資產可能仍可於本集團收
                         enforcement activities under the Group’s recovery                         回程序下被強制執行。撇銷構
                         procedures, taking into account legal advice where                        成終止確認事件。其後所收回
                         appropriate. A write-off constitutes a derecognition                      的任何款項於損益內確認。
                         event. Any subsequent recoveries are recognised in

     STATEMENTS AND SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES (continued)                要會計政策（續）
     3.2 Significant accounting policies (continued)                                    （續）
                                                                               3.2 主要會計政策
          Financial instruments (continued)                                         金融工具（續）
          Financial assets (continued)                                              金融資產（續）
          Impairment of financial assets (continued)                                     （續）
                                                                                    金融資產減值
          (v)   Measurement and recognition of ECL                                  (v)      預期信貸虧損的計量及確認
                The measurement of ECL is a function of the                                  預期信貸虧損的計量為違約概
                probability of default, loss given default (i.e. the                         率 、 違 約 損 失 率（即 存 在違 約
                magnitude of the loss if there is a default) and                             時 的 損 失 程 度）及 違 約 風險 的
                the exposure at default. The assessment of the                               函數。違約概率及違約損失率
                probability of default and loss given default is                             乃基於根據前瞻性資料和歷史
                based on historical data and forward-looking                                 數據評估。預期信貸虧損估計
                information. Estimation of ECL reflects an unbiased                          反映公正及機率加權金額，此
                and probability-weighted amount that is determined                           乃按相關違約發生風險為加權
                with the respective risks of default occurring as the                        而釐定。本集團採用撥備矩陣
                weights. The Group uses a practical expedient in                             估計貿易應收款項的預期信貸
                estimating ECL on trade receivables using a provision                        虧損，並考慮過往信貸虧損經
                matrix taking into consideration historical credit loss                      驗，並就不過多投入資金或精
                experience, adjusted for forward looking information                         力即可獲得的前瞻性資料而作
                that is available without undue cost or effort.                              調整。


                Generally, the ECL is the difference between all                             一般而言，預期信貸虧損為本
                contractual cash flows that are due to the Group                             集團根據合約應收的所有合約
                in accordance with the contract and the cash flows                           現金流量與本集團預計收取的
                that the Group expects to receive, discounted at                             現金流量之間的差額，並按初
                the effective interest rate determined at initial                            始確認時釐定的實際利率貼
                recognition.                                                                 現。


                Lifetime ECL for certain trade receivables are                               若干貿易應收款項的全期預期
                considered on a collective basis taking into                                 信貸虧損按逾期資料及相關信
                consideration past due information and relevant                              貸資料（如前瞻宏觀經濟資料）
                credit information such as forward looking                                   等綜合基準考量。
                macroeconomic information.

          Classification as debt or equity                                             分類為債務或權益
          Debt and equity instruments are classified as either financial               債務及權益工具乃根據合約安排之內
          liabilities or as equity in accordance with the substance                    容與金融負債及權益工具之定義分類
          of the contractual arrangements and the definitions of a                     為金融負債或權益。
          financial liability and an equity instrument.


          Equity instruments                                                           權益工具
          An equity instrument is any contract that evidences a                        權益工具為證明經扣除其所有負債後
          residual interest in the assets of an entity after deducting all             之實體資產剩餘權益之任何合約。由
          of its liabilities. Equity instruments issued by the Company                 本公司發行之權益工具乃按所收取所
          are recognised at the proceeds received, net of direct issue                 得款項（扣除直接發行成本）確認。
          costs.

          Financial liabilities (including trade payables, other                       金融負債（包括貿易應付款項、其他
          payables and accruals, and amounts due to ultimate                           應付款項及應計費用及應付最終控股
          holding company and fellow subsidiaries) are subsequently                    公司及同系附屬公司款項）其後將使
          measured at amortised cost, using the effective interest                     用實際利息法按攤銷成本計量。
          method.


          Derecognition of financial liabilities                                       終止確認金融負債
          The Group derecognises financial liabilities when, and only                  本集團於或僅於本集團之責任獲解

          have expired. The difference between the carrying amount                     債。所終止確認金融負債之賬面值與
          of the financial liability derecognised and the consideration                應付代價之差額於損益內確認。
          and payable is recognised in profit or loss.


          Changes in the basis for determining the contractual cash                    利率基準改革導致合同現金流量的釐

          For changes in the basis for determining the contractual                     對於採用攤銷成本計量的金融資產或

          on the carrying amount of the relevant financial asset or

           of economic benefit flowing back to the Group. After assessment,             本集團並無具有基金相關活動的可變回報，

     Estimated impairment of goodwill                                        商譽之估計減值
     Determining whether goodwill is impaired requires an estimation         釐定商譽有否減值須估計獲分配商譽之現金
     of the recoverable amount of the cash-generating unit (or group         產生單位（或現金產生單位組別）之可收回金
     of cash-generating units) to which goodwill has been allocated,         額，即使用價值或公平值減出售成本之較高
     which is the higher of the value in use or fair value less costs        者。計算使用價值須本集團估計預期現金產
     of disposal. The value in use calculation requires the Group to         生單位（或現金產生單位組別）將產生之未來
     estimate the future cash flows expected to arise from the cash-         現金流量及合適折現率，以計算現值。倘實
     generating unit (or a group of cash-generating units) and a             際未來現金流量低於預期，或事實及情況有
     suitable discount rate in order to calculate the present value.         變導致下調未來現金，則可能出現重大減值
     Where the actual future cash flows are less than expected, or           虧損。此外，由於Covid-19疫情可能發展及
     change in facts and circumstances which results in downward             演進且金融市場存在波動（包括本集團製造
     revision of future cash flows or upward revision of discount            及批發業務可能受阻），本年度的估計現金
     rate, a material impairment loss or further impairment loss may         流量及貼現率更大程度受估計不明朗因素所
     arise. Furthermore, the estimated cash flows and discount rate          影響。
     are subject to higher degree of estimation uncertainties in the
     current year due to uncertainty on how the Covid-19 pandemic
     may progress and evolve and volatility in financial markets,
     including potential disruptions of the Group’s manufacturing and
     wholesales operations.

     to the CGU. The cash flows beyond the five-year period are            之現金流使用穩定之3.0%（2020年：3.0%）
     extrapolated using a steady 3.0% (2020: 3.0%) per annum               年增長率推算。此增長率並未超過有關行業
     growth rate. This growth rate does not exceed the average long-       之平均長期增長率。使用價值計算之其他主
     term growth rate for the relevant industry. Other key assumptions     要假設及主要參數包括預算銷售額及毛利率
     and key parameters for the value in use calculation include           以及銷售價及成本之預期變動，乃根據管理
     budgeted sales and gross margin and expected changes to selling       層對市場發展及市場增長預測之預期而釐
     prices and cost, which are determined based on management’s           定。管理層認為，該等假設之任何合理可能
     expectations for the market development and market growth             變動概不會造成該現金產生單位之賬面總值
     forecasts. Management believes that any reasonably possible           超過可收回總金額。
     change in any of these assumptions would not cause the
     aggregate carrying amount of this CGU to exceed the aggregate
     recoverable amount of this unit.

      35. RETIREMENT BENEFITS SCHEME                                           35. 退休福利計劃
           The Group participates in both defined contribution scheme            本集團參與根據職業退休計劃條例註冊之定
           which is registered under the Occupational Retirement Schemes         額供款計劃（「職業退休計劃」）及於2000年
           Ordinance (the “ORSO Scheme”) and a Mandatory Provident               12月根據強制性公積金計劃條例成立之強
           Fund Scheme (the “MPF Scheme”) established under the                  制性公積金計劃（
                                                                                        「強積金計劃」
                                                                                              ）。該等計劃
           Mandatory Provident Fund Schemes Ordinance in December                之資產與本集團資產分開處理，由受託人管
           2000. The assets of the schemes are held separately from those        理之基金持有。在強積金計劃成立前為職業
           of the Group, in funds under the control of trustees. Certain         退休計劃成員之若干僱員仍然參與職業退休
           employees who were members of the ORSO Scheme prior to                計劃，對於在2000年12月1日或之後加入本
           the establishment of the MPF Scheme remained with the ORSO            集團之所有新僱員則必須參與強積金計劃。
           Scheme, whereas all new employees joining the Group on or
           after 1 December 2000 are required to join the MPF Scheme.


           For members of the MPF Scheme, the Group contributes 5%               就強積金計劃成員而言，本集團及其僱員各
           of relevant payroll costs to the Scheme, which contribution is        自須按僱員工資之5%向計劃作出供款，每
           matched by the employee and capped at HK$1,500 per month.             月供款上限為1,500港元。


           The ORSO Scheme is funded by monthly contributions from               職業退休計劃由僱員及本集團按僱員基本薪
           both employees and the Group at 5% of the employees’ basic            金之5%每月作出供款。自損益扣除之退休
           salary. The pension costs charged to profit or loss represent         金成本指本集團按該等計劃規定之比率已作
           contributions paid or payable to these funds by the Group at          出或應作出之供款。
           rates specified in the rules of these schemes.


           The employees of the Company’s subsidiaries in the PRC are            本公司之中國附屬公司僱員參與中國政府管
           members of the state-managed retirement benefits scheme               理之國家管理退休福利計劃。本公司中國附
           operated by the PRC government. The Company’s PRC                     屬公司須按僱員工資之一定百分比向該退休
           subsidiaries are required to contribute a certain percentage of       福利計劃供款，以撥付該項福利所需款項。
           their employees’ payroll costs to the retirement benefits scheme      本集團於該退休福利計劃內之唯一責任為根
           to fund the benefits. The only obligation of the Group with           據該計劃支付指定供款額。
           respect to the retirement benefits scheme is to make the required
           contributions under the scheme.


           The total expense recognised in profit or loss of HK$36,531,000       於損益確認之總開支36,531,000港元（2020
           (2020: HK$33,597,000) represents contributions payable to these       年：33,597,000港元）指本集團按計劃規則
           plans by the Group at rates specified in the rules of the plans.      所指定比例向該等計劃應作出供款。

      37. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                                 37. 資本風險管理
           The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the             本集團之資本管理旨在確保本集團旗下實體
           Group will be able to continue as a going concern while                  將能持續經營，同時透過經營產生之溢利為
           maximising the return to shareholders through the generation of          利益相關方爭取最高回報。本集團之整體策
           profits from its operations. The Group’s overall strategy remains        略較過往年度維持不變。
           unchanged from prior year.


           The capital structure of the Group consists of cash and cash             本集團資本結構包括現金及現金等價物及本
           equivalents and equity attributable to owners of the Company,            公司擁有人應佔權益（包括已發行股本及儲
           comprising issued share capital and reserves.                            備）。


           The directors of the Company review the capital structure on an          本公司董事按年度基準審閱資本結構。作為
           annual basis. As part of this review, the directors consider the         此審閱之一環，董事考慮資本成本及與資本
           cost of capital and the risks associated with its capital. Based on      相關之風險。本集團將根據董事之意見透過
           recommendations of the directors, the Group will balance its             支付股息及發行新股來平衡其整體資本結
           overall capital structure through the payment of dividends and           構。
           new share issues.

                 Credit risk and impairment assessment                                     信貸風險及減值評估
                 Credit risk refers to the risk that the Group’s counterparties            信貸風險指本集團對手方拖欠彼等之
                 default on their contractual obligations resulting in financial           合約責任導致本集團財務虧損的風
                 losses to the Group. The Group’s credit risk exposures are                險。本集團的信貸風險主要來自貿易
                 primarily attributable to trade receivables, amounts due                  應收款項、屬貿易性質之應收同系附
                 from fellow subsidiaries which are in trade nature, other                 屬公司款項、其他應收款項、應收貸
                 receivables, loan receivable, amount due from ultimate                    款、應收最終控股公司款項、銀行結
                 holding company, bank balances, time deposits with                        餘、三個月以上定期存款及一年以上
                 maturity over three months and time deposits with maturity                定期存款。本集團並無持有任何抵押
                 over one year. The Group does not hold any collateral                     品或其他信貸增級以填補與金融資產
                 or other credit enhancements to cover its credit risks                    有關的信貸風險，惟與應收貸款有關
                 associated with its financial assets, except that the credit              的信貸風險已降低，因為其以債務人
                 risks associated with loan receivable is mitigated because it             股東提供的擔保作抵押。
                 is secured over the guarantee provided by the shareholders
                 of the debtor.

           (c)   Fair value measurements of financial instruments                   (c)   金融工具公平值計量
                 Some of the Group’s financial instruments are measured                   本集團部分金融工具就財務呈報目的
                 at fair value for financial reporting purposes. The                      按公平值計量。管理層將就公平值計
                 management is to determine the appropriate valuation                     量釐定適合估值技術及輸入數據。
                 techniques and inputs for fair value measurements.


                 In estimating the fair value, the Group uses market-                     於估計公平值時，本集團採用其所得
                 observable data to the extent it is available. For instruments           市場可觀察數據。就存在重大不可
                 with significant unobservable inputs under Level 3, the                  觀察輸入數據的第三層工具而言，本
                 Group engages third party qualified valuers to perform                   集團委聘第三方合資格估值師進行估
                 the valuation. The management works closely with the                     值。管理層與合資格外聘估值師緊密
                 qualified external valuers to establish the appropriate                  合作設立模式適用之估值技術及輸入
                 valuation techniques and inputs to the model. The                        數據。管理層定期向本公司董事會匯
                 management reports the findings to the board of                          報結果以解釋公平值波動的原因。
                 directors of the Company regularly to explain the cause of
                 fluctuations in the fair value.


                 Fair value of the Group’s financial assets that are                      本集團按經常性基準以公平值計
                 measured at fair value on a recurring basis                              量之金融資產之公平值
                 Some of the Group’s financial assets are measured at fair                本集團若干金融資產於各報告期末按
                 value at the end of each reporting period. The following                 公平值計量。下表提供如何釐定該等
                 table gives information about how the fair values of these               金融資產之公平值（尤其是所用估值
                 financial assets are determined (in particular, the valuation            技術及輸入數據）之資料。
                 technique(s) and inputs used).

           a recurring basis (but fair value disclosures are                           之 公 平 值（ 惟 須 披 露 公 平 值 ）
           required)
           The directors of the Company consider that the carrying                     本公司董事認為，按攤銷成本在綜合
           amounts of financial assets and financial liabilities                       財務報表入賬之金融資產及金融負債
           recognised at amortised cost in the consolidated financial                  之賬面值與其公平值相若。
           statements approximate their fair values.


           The fair values of the financial assets and financial liabilities           上述金融資產及金融負債之公平值乃
           above have been determined in accordance with generally                     根據公認定價模式按折現現金流量分
           accepted pricing models based on a discounted cash flow                     析釐定。
           analysis.
